2026-10-12T06:33:40.4976440Z  	 c jsonb; r jsonb; ledger_state text; i record; t record;

2026-10-12T06:33:40.4978072Z  	 FROM "journal_migration_0ef26e5e3c604ce5a273fd88d1e9287f".workflow_instances wi
2026-10-12T06:33:40.4978417Z  	 JOIN "journal_migration_0ef26e5e3c604ce5a273fd88d1e9287f".workflow_definitions wd ON wd.app_id=wi.app_id AND wd.id=wi.flow_id

2026-10-12T06:33:40.4979264Z  	 WHERE wi.app_id=NEW.app_id AND wi.id=NEW.instance_id;

2026-10-12T06:33:40.4979664Z  	 OR (c->>'CommandID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.command_id

2026-10-12T06:33:40.4980626Z  	 OR (c->>'SchemaVersion')::bigint IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.schema_version
2026-10-12T06:33:40.4980854Z  	 OR (c->>'RecordVersion')::bigint IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.record_version
2026-10-12T06:33:40.4981128Z  	 OR (c->>'TableID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.table_id
2026-10-12T06:33:40.4981309Z  	 OR (c->>'ViewID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.view_id
2026-10-12T06:33:40.4981487Z  	 OR (c->>'RecordID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.record_id

2026-10-12T06:33:40.4982095Z  	 OR (c->>'DefinitionVersion')::bigint IS DISTINCT FROM i.definition_version

2026-10-12T06:33:40.4984912Z  	   RAISE EXCEPTION 'workflow journal terminal receipt mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23514';
2026-10-12T06:33:40.4985126Z  	  END IF;
2026-10-12T06:33:40.4985228Z  	 END IF;
2026-10-12T06:33:40.4985348Z  	 wanted_task:=nullif(c->>'TaskID','')::uuid;
2026-10-12T06:33:40.4985513Z  	 wanted_epoch:=(c->>'TaskEpoch')::bigint;
2026-10-12T06:33:40.4985687Z  	 wanted_target:=nullif(c->>'TargetNodeID','')::uuid;
2026-10-12T06:33:40.4985940Z  	 IF wanted_task IS NOT NULL THEN

2026-10-12T06:33:40.4986453Z  	  IF FOUND THEN

2026-10-12T06:33:40.5077203Z  	LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY INVOKER SET search_path=pg_catalog AS $$
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5077401Z  	DECLARE previous "round_migration_86305bb24f74439fbf05d3f0784db839".workflow_rounds%ROWTYPE; next_number bigint; parent_state text;
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5077448Z  	BEGIN
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5077543Z  	 IF current_setting('transaction_isolation')<>'read committed' THEN
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5077686Z  	  RAISE EXCEPTION USING ERRCODE='40001',MESSAGE='workflow round allocation requires read committed';
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5077785Z  	 END IF;
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5077999Z  	 PERFORM 1 FROM "round_migration_86305bb24f74439fbf05d3f0784db839".workflow_definitions WHERE app_id=NEW.app_id AND id=NEW.flow_id FOR UPDATE;
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5078156Z  	 IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION USING ERRCODE='23503',MESSAGE='workflow round definition missing'; END IF;
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5078288Z  	 SELECT * INTO previous FROM "round_migration_86305bb24f74439fbf05d3f0784db839".workflow_rounds
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5078397Z  	  WHERE app_id=NEW.app_id AND flow_id=NEW.flow_id AND record_id=NEW.record_id

2026-10-12T06:33:40.5166191Z  	DECLARE original_view uuid; original_version uuid;
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5166235Z  	BEGIN
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5166351Z  	 -- Row locking needs UPDATE on immutable versions, which runtime must not have.
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5166527Z  	 -- VOLATILE trigger statements acquire fresh RC snapshots after the shared lock.
2026-10-12T06:33:40.5166639Z  	 IF pg_catalog.current_setting('transaction_isolation') <> 'read committed' THEN
